The earlier tracker mixed status/state fields and marked unfinished work BLOCKED. This audit removes those conflicting fields, distinguishes implementation gaps from external blockers, and retains historical test narratives as historical evidence. No tests or sign-ins were started for this report.

## Current activity

No test, build or GitHub workflow is running for the redesign. Latest finished command: node --test --experimental-test-isolation=none tests/business-reminders.test.mjs; duration 1.349 seconds; 5 passed, 4 failed in the test loader (unresolved ./nonDomVisualTokens.mjs), before the worker assertions. Reminder SQL assertions passed on the isolated local database. Next is correcting the loader and running only this affected test.

## Remaining work by feature

- **UI:** Finish the remaining page/editor/dialog compositions and all-page loading/empty/error/copy review. Current phone substitution capture still shows header overlap; visual acceptance is incomplete.
- **MOB:** Finish matched phone/tablet/landscape review across all pages and editors, including fixed-header/footer spacing. Existing component browser passes are not full design acceptance.
- **GC:** Finish whole-business knowledge/action clause review and bounded real-provider checks on the final candidate; reuse unchanged language and memory evidence.
- **CHAT:** Existing optimistic submission, retry, IME and history regression passes; final combined release and live changed-path verification remain.
- **NL:** Matching, truthful totals and follow-up code/tests exist; verify actual service/photo queries and answer-first behavior on final candidate.
- **DATA:** Complete integration with remaining modules and authoritative arithmetic, then verify real permitted reads and unavailable-data behavior.
- **SCOPE:** Local two-business/role/forged-ID tests pass for implemented modules. Extend the same guards to remaining communications, waitlists and advice; verify final direct/follow-up denial before model access.
- **POL:** Complete browser edit/review/publish/refresh/public-link/booking-acknowledgement acceptance, failed-load recovery and final migrated live path.
- **BILL:** Reported subscription payment-method incident is not resolved. Safe diagnostics exist; identify the specific Stripe portal failure and verify correct existing customer, no app-initiated charge, default-method readback and cancellation.
- **FIN:** Finish detailed finance clause reconciliation, offline-sale client attribution, daily close, complete breakdown/detail views and translated labels/exports; final role/live acceptance remains. Existing operating books, compensation, exports and stock-sale logic have local passes.
- **PROMO:** Eligible-subtotal calculation, unchanged applicable deposit, unpaid-balance cap and immutable booked terms have local coverage. Finish management-page/details and full public offer/checkout/live acceptance.
- **COPY:** Complete platform-wide plain-language copy review in four languages; do not confuse translated static source catalogs with verified complete screens.
- **AVATAR:** Selection/persistence API and UI code exist; verify all requested avatar choices and consistent launcher/header/message use across devices and sessions.
- **VALUE:** Complete remaining business modules and four-language coverage; verify deposit routing/no platform commission against actual existing Stripe flow without making a real charge.
- **CLIENT:** Private cards, own-business guest-history links and field-scoped access have local SQL/server/browser evidence. Finish offline-sale client spend association and final live role/persistence verification.
- **ADVISE:** Implement the six evidence-grounded opportunity calculations and approved next actions; no invented profit/availability/client figures. No complete dedicated advice module is verified.
- **COMMS:** Finish reminder revision worker tests, opt-in delivery preferences, post-visit thank-you/rebooking, consent-aware own-client campaigns and remaining reusable/attachment capabilities. Substitution and conversation lifecycle have local passes. Missing separate conversation document is recorded; explicitly specified clauses remain required.
- **NOSHOW:** Own-business incident protection and protected deposits have local SQL/browser tests. No cross-business signal is implemented or authorized. Final live role/customer-correction verification remains.
- **WAIT:** Implement appointment waitlist preferences, cancellation offers, expiration, atomic acceptance and current availability/deposit checks. This is not the removed future-category marketing waitlist.
- **STOCK:** Stock reconciliation, supplies, movements, sales deductions and optional costs/assets have local SQL/server/browser evidence. Final workflow and role/live verification remain.
- **EXPENSE:** Expense records and stock purchases have local evidence; complete final finance integration and live authorized persistence/role verification.
- **PUBLIC:** Code includes real /site-access entry, removal of public demo gates/banner, supported-category/navigation corrections and component polish. Complete every detailed public clause, full booking path, matched responsive before/after review and production cache/banner/route checks; root landing/content must remain.
- **PAGE:** Complete this page and all its detail/edit/dialog states against the reference, four languages and actual feature clauses; component passes alone do not close the page requirement.
- **EXEC:** Complete remaining evidence mapping, final diff review, required PR/main CI, protected pending migrations, stable held candidate checks, authorized publication and actual public verification.

### SCOPE-01 — In progress

SCOPE-01 — The assistant serves the currently selected, authorized business only, plus general Girlz Culture platform guidance. It must refuse requests for information about another business, including publicly available profiles, prices, services, reviews or images. A public webpage is not an exception. Do not browse or query other businesses to satisfy such requests.

**Acceptance:** AUTOMATED ONLY. **Release:** NOT RELEASED.

**Written:** Current-business services/media reads and platform-guidance allowlist; no foreign ID/URL accepted. Finance permission downgrade and stylist reassignment now discard broader stored results and derived transcript before planning. Idempotent read replay retrieves fresh authorized data. Raw request/audit REST history is backend-only. Current-scope own-earnings saves are separately guarded. Demonstrated missing/foreign prior request IDs could leave client transcript prose in provider context. Planner now drops associated prose unless every requested ID resolves under the current business, actor and permissions.

**Evidence:** API and execution fixtures include two separate businesses Planning33/33,execution15/15; full157-migration disposable local chain and rollback SQL two-business/role/replay guards passed. Production unchanged; live isolation still pending. Fail-before / pass-after regression for entirely foreign and mixed history. All 34 planner tests pass, preserving authorized clarification and language continuity.

**Remaining:** Local two-business/role/forged-ID tests pass for implemented modules. Extend the same guards to remaining communications, waitlists and advice; verify final direct/follow-up denial before model access.

**Acceptance:** AUTOMATED ONLY. **Release:** NOT RELEASED.

**Written:** Versioned owner-set deposits use the highest applicable rate once; price thresholds and verified own-business incidents only. Immutable booking snapshots preserve old terms. ### FIN-18 — In progress

FIN-18 — Keep agreed sale value, completed service/product sales, cash received, unpaid balances, expenses and recorded profit distinct. Define each reporting period and calculation consistently. Discounts and refunds reduce the relevant amounts once; stylist compensation and recorded payouts must not count as the same expense twice. Inventory restock costs and the cost attributed to sold products need a consistent treatment to avoid deducting both twice.

**Acceptance:** AUTOMATED ONLY. **Release:** NOT RELEASED.

**Written:** Added protected operating-book records/RPCs, idempotent external receipts and refund links, versioned compensation and obligations, owner/full/own-stylist/logging scopes; new ledger-backed finance screen and assistant summaries. Existing Unpaid substring bug corrected. These foundations do not complete all detailed clauses. Verified online merchandise/orders/refunds now join the same scoped ledger with separate tax/shipping. Added immutable commission/wage/booth arrangements, period obligations, paid/outstanding/advance positions, mobile record cards, balance/refund entry, date presets and retained idempotent retry. No charges or transfers. Unknown historic costs remain explicit. Own-business retail/supply stock, configurable thresholds, restock/usage/correction with revision and request guards, immutable movement history; offline catalog product sales deduct once; existing reservation/release/import paths reconcile. Optional retail purchase cost is inventory_asset; supply purchase is operating expense. Existing catalog edits and imports cannot restore stale quantities.

**Evidence:** 156-migration chain passed against an empty local PostgreSQL17.11 fixture, including two-business and role SQL assertions. Finance arithmetic10/10, data-boundary6/6 and route5/5 tests; operational assistant18/18 and planning31/31. No production migration applied. Core12/12,adapter10/10,routes5/5; finance browser12/12 Chromium/WebKit including390,768,1440,landscape and wage100/payment40/outstanding60. Local157 SQL chain passed; final/live gates pending. 162-migration clean database replay and two-business inventory SQL; real reserve/release retries, stale import, failed and duplicate offline sales, permissions. 24/24 inventory+finance browser cases across EN/FR/ES/zh-CN, Chromium/WebKit; 33 Node finance/core/route cases and 20 operational assistant cases. Build/types/lint pass. A product named Save was incorrectly translated in finance display. Business product/client/stylist names now excluded from interface translation; four-language reload evidence passes.

**Remaining:** Finish detailed finance clause reconciliation, offline-sale client attribution, daily close, complete breakdown/detail views and translated labels/exports; final role/live acceptance remains. Existing operating books, compensation, exports and stock-sale logic have local passes.

Reviewed attendance events support corrections and customer/secure-guest review requests; disputed incidents are excluded from increased deposits. Assigned staff and guest token scope enforced before reads and writes. Checkout requires exact current total/deposit acknowledgment; revised terms retain guest details but clear financial consent. Zero-deposit genuine bookings are live records with origin snapshot, without creating a charge.

Current rerun also covers mobile nav changes.

## Follow-up after the requested snapshot

The four reminder integration failures were loader failures. Once fixed, a regression exposed translated email variables being removed before substitution. This application defect was corrected; 17 reminder/substitution checks now pass, including four-language factual email/link preservation. TypeScript, focused lint, production acceptance build, existing reminder verifier and the fresh 167-migration chain pass. Earlier snapshot counts remain conservative whole-requirement states; post-visit/preferences and live delivery acceptance remain unfinished.
